### [1] [Computational Foundations for Strategic Coopetition: Formalizing Sequential Interaction and Reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01240)
*Vik Pant,Eric Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文构建了多利益相关者系统中战略竞合的序列交互计算框架，将i*概念建模与博弈论互惠分析相结合，提出了四种核心机制，并通过大规模参数配置和苹果iOS应用商店生态系统实证验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多利益相关者系统中，战略竞合需要理解在缺乏约束性合同的情况下合作如何随时间持续存在。现有研究缺乏连接概念建模与博弈论互惠分析的计算基础，特别是在序列交互动态方面存在理论空白。

Method: 提出了四种核心机制：(1)有界互惠响应函数，将伙伴偏离映射为有限条件响应；(2)记忆窗口历史追踪，捕获k个最近周期的认知限制；(3)结构互惠敏感性，从相互依赖矩阵导出，行为响应被结构依赖放大；(4)信任门控互惠，信任调节互惠响应。框架适用于人类利益相关者交互和多智能体计算系统。

Result: 在15,625个参数配置的全面验证中，六个行为目标均超过阈值：合作涌现(97.5%)、背叛惩罚(100%)、宽恕动态(87.9%)、不对称分化(100%)、信任-互惠交互(100%)、有界响应(100%)。使用苹果iOS应用商店生态系统(2008-2024)的实证验证达到43/51个适用点(84.3%)，成功重现了五个生态系统阶段的合作模式。统计显著性p < 0.001，Cohen's d = 1.57。

Conclusion: 本研究完成了基础系列(TR-1至TR-4)，采用单轴处理方法，即智能体沿着单一连续统选择合作水平。配套工作已在相互依赖、信任和集体行动方面发表。扩展系列(TR-5至TR-8)将引入双轴处理，将合作和竞争视为独立维度。

Abstract: Strategic coopetition in multi-stakeholder systems requires understanding how cooperation persists through time without binding contracts. This technical report extends computational foundations for strategic coopetition to sequential interaction dynamics, bridging conceptual modeling (i* framework) with game-theoretic reciprocity analysis. We develop: (1) bounded reciprocity response functions mapping partner deviations to finite conditional responses, (2) memory-windowed history tracking capturing cognitive limitations over k recent periods, (3) structural reciprocity sensitivity derived from interdependence matrices where behavioral responses are amplified by structural dependencies, and (4) trust-gated reciprocity where trust modulates reciprocity responses. The framework applies to both human stakeholder interactions and multi-agent computational systems. Comprehensive validation across 15,625 parameter configurations demonstrates robust reciprocity effects, with all six behavioral targets exceeding thresholds: cooperation emergence (97.5%), defection punishment (100%), forgiveness dynamics (87.9%), asymmetric differentiation (100%), trust-reciprocity interaction (100%), and bounded responses (100%). Empirical validation using the Apple iOS App Store ecosystem (2008-2024) achieves 43/51 applicable points (84.3%), reproducing documented cooperation patterns across five ecosystem phases. Statistical significance confirmed at p < 0.001 with Cohen's d = 1.57. This report concludes the Foundations Series (TR-1 through TR-4) adopting uniaxial treatment where agents choose cooperation levels along a single continuum. Companion work on interdependence (arXiv:2510.18802), trust (arXiv:2510.24909), and collective action (arXiv:2601.16237) has been prepublished. Extensions Series (TR-5 through TR-8) introduces biaxial treatment where cooperation and competition are independent dimensions.

</details>


### [2] [Free Information Disrupts Even Bayesian Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01838)
*Jonas Stein,Shannon Cruz,Davide Grossi,Martina Testori*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 即使是在理想条件下，不受限制的信息交流也可能损害群体信念的正确性，因此在设计社交媒体等信息网络时需要慎重考虑信息流动的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代信息网络（如社交媒体平台）的核心原则之一是用户在交换信息时不应受到限制。作者旨在挑战这一假设，探究不受限制的信息交流是否会对群体信念的正确性产生负面影响。

Method: 采用基于计算主体的模型（computational agent-based model），模拟了一群具有真值寻求动机、合作精神且具备完美信息处理能力的理想化智能体，研究其在无约束信息交换条件下的表现。

Result: 即使在理想化的智能体群体中，不受限制的信息交换也可能导致群体信念正确性受到损害。这表明在实践中，不受限的信息流可能同样会产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 不应盲目假设信息交流越自由越好。在设计具有重大社会影响的通信网络（如社交媒体平台）时，应审慎考虑对信息流动施加适当的约束。

Abstract: A core tenet underpinning the conception of contemporary information networks, such as social media platforms, is that users should not be constrained in the amount of information they can freely and willingly exchange with one another about a given topic. By means of a computational agent-based model, we show how even in groups of truth-seeking and cooperative agents with perfect information-processing abilities, unconstrained information exchange may lead to detrimental effects on the correctness of the group's beliefs. If unconstrained information exchange can be detrimental even among such idealized agents, it is prudent to assume it can also be so in practice. We therefore argue that constraints on information flow should be carefully considered in the design of communication networks with substantial societal impact, such as social media platforms.

</details>


### [3] [Optimizing Interventions for Agent-Based Infectious Disease Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02016)
*Anja Wolpers,Johannes Ponge,Adelinde M. Uhrmacher*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADIOS系统，利用语法引导遗传编程（GGGP）优化非药物干预措施，以在代理-based流行病学模拟中自动生成最优干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏药物选择时，非药物干预措施是控制传染病传播的常用工具，但识别最小化社会干扰的有效干预措施仍然具有挑战性。代理-based模拟是分析流行病学中可能干预措施影响的流行工具，但自动优化NPIs使用代理-based模拟是一个复杂问题，因为干预措施可以基于多个属性针对个体，影响分层群体结构（如学校、工作场所和家庭），并且可以任意组合，导致搜索空间非常大甚至无限。

Method: 开发了Agent-based Infectious Disease Intervention Optimization System (ADIOS)，其核心是一个特定领域语言，通过上下文无关文法构建干预搜索空间。通过定义约束条件防止生成语义无效的干预模式，进一步减少搜索空间。ADIOS采用Grammar-Guided Genetic Programming (GGGP)方法进行基于模拟的优化，并提供与代理-based模拟的耦合接口。

Result: 使用German Epidemic Micro-Simulation System (GEMS)作为案例研究，展示了该方法为现实流行病学模型生成最优干预措施的潜力。系统能够有效处理大规模搜索空间，并通过约束条件提高优化效率。

Conclusion: ADIOS系统成功地将语法引导遗传编程应用于非药物干预措施优化，为决策者提供了一个有效工具，能够在复杂的代理-based流行病学模型中自动生成最优干预策略，同时最小化社会干扰。

Abstract: Non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) are commonly used tools for controlling infectious disease transmission when pharmaceutical options are unavailable. Yet, identifying effective interventions that minimize societal disruption remains challenging. Agent-based simulation is a popular tool for analyzing the impact of possible interventions in epidemiology. However, automatically optimizing NPIs using agent-based simulations poses a complex problem because, in agent-based epidemiological models, interventions can target individuals based on multiple attributes, affect hierarchical group structures (e.g., schools, workplaces, and families), and be combined arbitrarily, resulting in a very large or even infinite search space. We aim to support decision-makers with our Agent-based Infectious Disease Intervention Optimization System (ADIOS) that optimizes NPIs for infectious disease simulations using Grammar-Guided Genetic Programming (GGGP). The core of ADIOS is a domain-specific language for expressing NPIs in agent-based simulations that structures the intervention search space through a context-free grammar. To make optimization more efficient, the search space can be further reduced by defining constraints that prevent the generation of semantically invalid intervention patterns. Using this constrained language and an interface that enables coupling with agent-based simulations, ADIOS adopts the GGGP approach for simulation-based optimization. Using the German Epidemic Micro-Simulation System (GEMS) as a case study, we demonstrate the potential of our approach to generate optimal interventions for realistic epidemiological models

</details>

### [4] [Trustworthy AI-Driven Dynamic Hybrid RIS: Joint Optimization and Reward Poisoning-Resilient Control in Cognitive MISO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01238)
*Deemah H. Tashman,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能量自适应的混合可重构智能表面(RIS)方案，并利用软演员-评论家(SAC)深度强化学习算法解决了波束成形和相位优化问题，同时首次研究了针对DRL智能体的奖励投毒攻击并提出了有效防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络中次级用户(SU)直连链路不可靠且存在能量限制，而现有研究多采用静态RIS架构，无法动态适应能量可用性的变化。此外，DRL在RIS增强认知无线电网络中的安全性（特别是奖励投毒攻击）尚未得到系统性研究。

Method: 1. 提出自适应混合RIS架构，可根据收集能量实时在无源和有源模式间切换；2. 在考虑硬件损伤和级联衰落信道的实际场景下建模；3. 利用SAC深度强化学习算法联合优化发射波束成形和RIS相位；4. 首次系统研究RIS增强CRN中DRL智能体的奖励投毒攻击，并提出基于奖励裁剪和统计异常过滤的轻量级实时防御方案。

Result: 1. SAC方法在连续和高度动态环境中显著优于现有DRL基线算法；2. 动态混合RIS在吞吐量和能耗之间实现了优于全无源和全有源方案的性能权衡；3. 所提防御机制在对抗条件下能有效维持次级用户性能。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了RIS辅助认知无线电网络的实用化和安全化部署，为能量受限无线系统提供了关键设计洞察，证明了混合RIS架构和DRL安全机制在下一代无线网络中的重要价值。

Abstract: Cognitive radio networks (CRNs) are a key mechanism for alleviating spectrum scarcity by enabling secondary users (SUs) to opportunistically access licensed frequency bands without harmful interference to primary users (PUs). To address unreliable direct SU links and energy constraints common in next-generation wireless networks, this work introduces an adaptive, energy-aware hybrid reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) for underlay multiple-input single-output (MISO) CRNs. Distinct from prior approaches relying on static RIS architectures, our proposed RIS dynamically alternates between passive and active operation modes in real time according to harvested energy availability. We also model our scenario under practical hardware impairments and cascaded fading channels. We formulate and solve a joint transmit beamforming and RIS phase optimization problem via the soft actor-critic (SAC) deep reinforcement learning (DRL) method, leveraging its robustness in continuous and highly dynamic environments. Notably, we conduct the first systematic study of reward poisoning attacks on DRL agents in RIS-enhanced CRNs, and propose a lightweight, real-time defense based on reward clipping and statistical anomaly filtering. Numerical results demonstrate that the SAC-based approach consistently outperforms established DRL baselines, and that the dynamic hybrid RIS strikes a superior trade-off between throughput and energy consumption compared to fully passive and fully active alternatives. We further show the effectiveness of our defense in maintaining SU performance even under adversarial conditions. Our results advance the practical and secure deployment of RIS-assisted CRNs, and highlight crucial design insights for energy-constrained wireless systems.

</details>


### [5] [ML-Enabled Open RAN: A Comprehensive Survey of Architectures, Challenges, and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01239)
*Mira Chandra Kirana,Patatchona Keyela,Fatemeh Rostamian,Deemah H. Tashman,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器学习在开放式无线接入网（O-RAN）中的集成应用，阐述了其在频谱管理、资源分配和安全等方面提升网络性能的关键作用，并为未来研究指明了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无线通信系统的进步，O-RAN作为促进互操作性和成本效益的框架备受关注。面对频谱管理、资源分配和安全等挑战，机器学习展现出巨大的解决潜力，因此亟需对其在O-RAN中的应用现状进行全面梳理。

Method: 通过系统分析现有文献，对O-RAN架构演进及其基本原理进行考察，总结并归纳了机器学习在O-RAN各应用场景中的集成方法与实践。

Result: 提供了机器学习在O-RAN中应用的全面综述，揭示了其在增强网络性能和效率方面的变革性潜力，并指出了当前研究的热点与空白。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于研究人员和利益相关者制定最佳服务策略，推动对智能无线网络的深入理解，并为未来的研究探索提供了明确的指引方向。

Abstract: As wireless communication systems become more advanced, Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) stand out as a notable framework that promotes interoperability and cost-effectiveness. An examination of the progression of RAN architectures, as well as O-RAN's underlying principles, reveals the importance of machine learning (ML) in addressing various challenges, including spectrum management, resource allocation, and security. Hence, this survey provides a comprehensive overview of the integration of ML within O-RAN, highlighting its transformative potential in enhancing network performance and efficiency. This survey aims to describe the current status of ML applications in O-RAN while indicating possible directions for future research by analyzing existing literature. The findings aim to assist researchers and stakeholders in formulating optimal service strategies and advancing the understanding of intelligent wireless networks.

</details>


### [6] [Quantum Networking Fundamentals: From Physical Protocols to Network Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01910)
*Athanasios Gkelias,Felix T. A. Burt,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本教程提出软件定义量子网络（SDQN）架构，弥合量子物理与经典网络社区的鸿沟，解决量子互联网从实验室走向可扩展基础设施的编排挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子互联网在安全通信、分布式量子计算等领域具有变革性潜力，但从实验室实验过渡到可扩展的多租户网络基础设施面临深层编排挑战。目前开发工作在物理社区内部孤立进行，优先考虑硬件，而经典网络社区缺乏管理脆弱量子资源的架构模型。

Method: 建立软件定义量子网络（SDQN）作为扩展前提，采用双平面架构使经典控制主导量子数据流；构建SDQN参考模型和量子网络操作系统（QNOS）实现硬件抽象；采用量子网络效用最大化（Q-NUM）框架作为统一数学视角，用于分析纠缠路由、调度和保真度之间的权衡。

Result: 消除了当前模拟器中的理想化假设，将其转化为显式控制平面约束以解决"模拟-现实鸿沟"；以分布式量子人工智能（DQAI）在不完美网络上的部署为案例，揭示了概率性延迟节点和退相干等物理约束如何决定应用层可行性。

Conclusion: 为网络工程师提供了必要工具，推动量子网络从定制的物理实验转变为可编程、多租户的全球基础设施，实现量子网络研究的范式转变。

Abstract: The realization of the Quantum Internet promises transformative capabilities in secure communication, distributed quantum computing, and high-precision metrology. However, transitioning from laboratory experiments to a scalable, multi-tenant network utility introduces deep orchestration challenges. Current development is often siloed within physics communities, prioritizing hardware, while the classical networking community lacks architectural models to manage fragile quantum resources. This tutorial bridges this divide by providing a network-centric view of quantum networking. We dismantle idealized assumptions in current simulators to address the "simulation-reality gap," recasting them as explicit control-plane constraints. To bridge this gap, we establish Software-Defined Quantum Networking (SDQN) as a prerequisite for scale, prioritizing a symbiotic, dual-plane architecture where classical control dictates quantum data flow. Specifically, we synthesize reference models for SDQN and the Quantum Network Operating System (QNOS) for hardware abstraction, and adapt a Quantum Network Utility Maximization (Q-NUM) framework as a unifying mathematical lens for engineers to reason about trade-offs between entanglement routing, scheduling, and fidelity. Furthermore, we analyze Distributed Quantum AI (DQAI) over imperfect networks as a case study, illustrating how physical constraints such as probabilistic stragglers and decoherence dictate application-layer viability. Ultimately, this tutorial equips network engineers with the tools required to transition quantum networking from a bespoke physics experiment into a programmable, multi-tenant global infrastructure.

</details>


### [7] [Physics-Informed Transformer for Multi-Band Channel Frequency Response Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01944)
*Anatolij Zubow,Joana Angjo,Sigrid Dimce,Falko Dressler*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息复数Transformer的方法，用于在存在共信道干扰导致频谱片段缺失的情况下，从部分观测的频谱快照重建完整的宽带信道频率响应（CFR）。该方法在干扰占用率高达50%时仍能实现优异的PDP相似度（ρ≥0.82），显著优于传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多频带无线系统中，当一个或多个子频带被共信道干扰暂时阻塞时，宽带信道频率响应（CFR）的估计极具挑战性。现有的传统插值方法在处理这种碎片化、部分观测的频谱数据时性能受限，需要一种能够有效重建完整宽带CFR的新方法。

Method: 1. 使用独立的两状态离散时间马尔可夫链建模每个子频带的干扰模式，捕捉真实的突发占用行为。
2. 构建复数Transformer架构，在T个快照和F个频率_bin_的联合时频网格上运行，采用分解自注意力机制分别沿时间和频率轴进行注意力计算，将计算复杂度降低至O(TF² + FT²)。
3. 通过全纯线性层处理复数值输入输出，保持相位关系。
4. 训练采用复合物理信息损失函数，结合频谱保真度、功率时延谱(PDP)重建、信道冲激响应(CIR)稀疏性和时间平滑性。
5. 通过每个样本的速度随机化融入移动性效应，实现对不同移动场景的泛化。

Result: 与三种经典基线方法（最后观测值前向携带、零填充、三次样条插值）相比，该方法在干扰占用率高达50%时实现了最高的PDP相似度，达到ρ≥0.82，而最佳基线仅达到ρ≥0.62。此外，模型在全速度范围内平滑退化，一致性地优于所有其他基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的物理信息复数Transformer能够有效解决多频带系统中频谱片段缺失条件下的宽带CFR估计问题，在干扰场景下实现显著的性能提升，为实际无线通信系统中的信道估计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Wideband channel frequency response (CFR) estimation is challenging in multi-band wireless systems, especially when one or more sub-bands are temporarily blocked by co-channel interference. We present a physics-informed complex Transformer that reconstructs the full wideband CFR from such fragmented, partially observed spectrum snapshots. The interference pattern in each sub-band is modeled as an independent two-state discrete-time Markov chain, capturing realistic bursty occupancy behavior. Our model operates on the joint time-frequency grid of $T$ snapshots and $F$ frequency bins and uses a factored self-attention mechanism that separately attends along both axes, reducing the computational complexity to $O(TF^2 + FT^2)$. Complex-valued inputs and outputs are processed through a holomorphic linear layer that preserves phase relationships. Training uses a composite physics-informed loss combining spectral fidelity, power delay profile (PDP) reconstruction, channel impulse response (CIR) sparsity, and temporal smoothness. Mobility effects are incorporated through per-sample velocity randomization, enabling generalization across different mobility regimes. Evaluation against three classical baselines, namely, last-observation-carry-forward, zero-fill, and cubic-spline interpolation, shows that our approach achieves the highest PDP similarity with respect to the ground truth, reaching $ρ\geq 0.82$ compared to $ρ\geq 0.62$ for the best baseline at interference occupancy levels up to 50%. Furthermore, the model degrades smoothly across the full velocity range, consistently outperforming all other baselines.

</details>


### [8] [CIVIC: Cooperative Immersion Via Intelligent Credit-sharing in DRL-Powered Metaverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02284)
*Amr Aboeleneen,Mohamed Abdallah,Aiman Erbad,Amr Salem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CIVIC框架，通过在多个元宇宙服务提供商（MSP）之间建立合作机制与信用共享池，利用深度强化学习优化资源分配，显著提升了请求完成率、满足率与公平性，有效解决了元宇宙中的复杂资源分配挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元宇宙场景中存在多样化的虚拟环境、数字孪生、动态用户需求以及严格的沉浸式体验要求，导致资源分配极为复杂，现有方法难以有效应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出了CIVIC（Cooperative Immersion Via Intelligent Credit-sharing）框架，集成了虚拟环境渲染、数字孪生同步、信用共享和沉浸式感知资源配置。将资源分配问题建模为两个NP难问题：非合作博弈与基于通用信用池（GCP）的 cooperative setting。采用深度强化学习（DRL）进行资源调优与管理合作MSP。

Result: 实验结果表明，CIVIC相比现有方法实现了12-36%更高的请求完成率、23-70%更高的满足率、服务客户数量增加20-60%，以及请求分配公平性提升达51%，同时保持了具有竞争力的成本。在动态负载和突发需求下表现出良好的韧性、适应性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CIVIC框架通过多MSP合作与智能信用共享机制，有效解决了元宇宙中的资源分配难题，在性能与公平性上均取得了显著提升，适用于现实世界的分布式元宇宙基础设施。

Abstract: The Metaverse faces complex resource allocation challenges due to diverse Virtual Environments (VEs), Digital Twins (DTs), dynamic user demands, and strict immersion needs. This paper introduces CIVIC (Cooperative Immersion Via Intelligent Credit-sharing), a novel framework optimizing resource sharing among multiple Metaverse Service Providers (MSPs) to enhance user immersion. Unlike existing methods, CIVIC integrates VE rendering, DT synchronization, credit sharing, and immersion-aware provisioning within a cooperative multi-MSP model. The resource allocation problem is formulated as two NP-hard challenges: a non-cooperative setting where MSPs operate independently and a cooperative setting utilizing a General Credit Pool (GCP) for dynamic resource sharing. Using Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for tuning resources and managing cooperating MSPs, CIVIC achieves 12-36% higher request completion, 23-70% higher fulfillment rates, 20-60% more served clients, and up to 51% more fairly distributed requests, all with competitive costs. Extensive experiments demonstrate CIVIC's resilience, adaptability, and robust performance under dynamic load conditions and unexpected demand surges, making it suitable for real-world distributed Metaverse infrastructures.

</details>

### [9] [EXaCTz: Guaranteed Extremum Graph and Contour Tree Preservation for Distributed- and GPU-Parallel Lossy Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01397)
*Yuxiao Li,Mingze Xia,Xin Liang,Bei Wang,Hanqi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EXaCTz，一种并行算法，在保持极值图和轮廓树拓扑结构的同时，将拓扑修正吞吐量提升至最高4.52 GB/s（单GPU），相比现有最优方法加速高达3,285倍，并提供了理论收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的误差有界有损压缩技术在保持拓扑结构时存在三大问题：（1）拓扑修正速度（MB/s级）远落后于压缩速度（GB/s级），存在显著的吞吐量差距；（2）对多样化拓扑描述符的支持有限；（3）缺乏理论收敛边界。

Method: EXaCTz提出了一种高性能、有界迭代的并行算法。不同于依赖显式拓扑重建的现有方法，该算法通过强制所有顶点的一致性最小/最大邻居关系以及关键点之间的全局排序来确保拓扑一致性。算法证明了收敛性，其迭代次数上限由脆弱性图中的最长路径决定，该图刻画了修正过程中的潜在级联效应。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，EXaCTz单GPU吞吐量达4.52 GB/s，相比现有最优方法（Gorski et al.）加速高达213倍（单核CPU对比）和3,285倍（单GPU对比）。在分布式环境下，算法可扩展至128个GPU，效率达55.6%（对比朴素并行化的6.4%），能在48秒内处理512 GB数据集，聚合修正吞吐量达32.69 GB/s。

Conclusion: EXaCTz成功解决了有损压缩数据中拓扑保持的吞吐量瓶颈问题，首次实现了极值图和轮廓树的联合保持，并提供了理论收敛保证，为大规模科学模拟和工作流中的拓扑保持压缩提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces EXaCTz, a parallel algorithm that concurrently preserves extremum graphs and contour trees in lossy-compressed scalar field data. While error-bounded lossy compression is essential for large-scale scientific simulations and workflows, existing topology-preserving methods suffer from (1) a significant throughput disparity, where topology correction speeds are on the order of MB/s, lagging orders of magnitude behind compression speeds on the order of GB/s, (2) limited support for diverse topological descriptors, and (3) a lack of theoretical convergence bounds. To address these challenges, EXaCTz introduces a high-performance, bounded-iteration algorithm that enforces topological consistency by deriving targeted edits for decompressed data. Unlike prior methods that rely on explicit topology reconstruction, EXaCTz enforces consistent min/max neighbors of all vertices, along with global ordering among critical points. As such, the algorithm enforces consistent critical-point classification, saddle extremum connectivity, and the preservation of merge/split events. We theoretically prove the convergence of our algorithm, bounded by the longest path in a vulnerability graph that characterizes potential cascading effects during correction. Experiments on real-world datasets show that EXaCTz achieves a single-GPU throughput of up to 4.52 GB/s, outperforming the state-of-the-art contour-tree-preserving method (Gorski et al.) by up to 213x (with a single-core CPU implementation for fair comparison) and 3,285x (with a single-GPU version). In distributed environments, EXaCTz scales to 128 GPUs with 55.6\% efficiency (compared with 6.4\% for a naive parallelization), processing datasets of up to 512 GB in under 48 seconds and achieving an aggregate correction throughput of up to 32.69 GB/s.

</details>


### [10] [ModTrans: Translating Real-world Models for Distributed Training Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01607)
*Yi Lyu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ModTrans工具，将真实模型转换为ASTRA-sim模拟器输入格式，解决了现有分布式训练模拟器不支持真实模型的问题，且转换开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式训练是机器学习系统的研究热点，但在缺乏物理机器和资源的条件下进行实验十分困难。现有的分布式训练模拟器（如ASTRA-sim）不支持导入真实世界开发的模型，这为机器学习研究人员使用这些模拟器带来了障碍。

Method: 开发了ModTrans，一种支持将任意真实世界模型转换为ASTRA-sim模拟器输入格式的翻译器，从而消除机器学习专家与机器学习系统研究人员之间的隔阂。

Result: 实验结果表明，ModTrans的转换成本（开销）可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: ModTrans成功搭建了真实模型与分布式训练模拟器之间的桥梁，使研究人员能够在无需物理资源的情况下，利用模拟器对实际开发的模型进行实验分析。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed training has been a research hot spot in machine learning systems for industry and academia in recent years. However, conducting experiments without physical machines and corresponding resources is difficult. One solution is to leverage distributed training simulators, but current ones like ASTRA-sim do not support importing real-world developed models, which poses challenges for ML researchers seeking to use them. Based on this challenge, we developed ModTrans, a translator supporting format translation from any real-world model to the ASTRA-sim simulator's input, removing the barrier between machine learning experts and machine learning system researchers. The experiment results show that ModTrans's cost is negligible.

</details>


### [11] [DWDP: Distributed Weight Data Parallelism for High-Performance LLM Inference on NVL72](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01621)
*Wanqian Li,Jintao Peng,Zongfei Jing,Tianyu Zhang,Ze Long,Xianjie Qiao,Xiaoming Chen,Dongxu Yang,Kefeng Duan,June Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DWDP（分布式权重数据并行）策略，通过消除层级间同步并支持按需获取MoE专家，在DeepSeek-R1模型上实现了8.8%的端到端性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型推理并行化策略需要在每一层进行跨秩同步，这使得端到端性能对工作负载不均衡非常敏感，限制了推理效率和GPU利用率。

Method: 提出DWDP并行化策略，保持数据并行执行的同时将MoE权重分散到对等GPU上，按需获取缺失的专家。通过消除集体跨秩同步，使每个GPU能够独立执行。同时引入分裂权重管理和异步远程权重预取两项优化技术来降低实际开销。

Result: 在TensorRT-LLM上实现，使用DeepSeek-R1模型在GB200 NVL72平台上评估。在8K输入序列长度和1K输出序列长度下，于20-100 TPS/用户的服务范围内，DWDP在保持相当的每用户吞吐量条件下，将端到端输出TPS/GPU提升了8.8%。

Conclusion: DWDP通过去除层级间同步约束，有效解决了现有并行策略中的负载不均衡问题，为大规模LLM推理提供了一种更高效的并行化方案，显著提升了GPU利用率和推理性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference increasingly depends on multi-GPU execution, yet existing inference parallelization strategies require layer-wise inter-rank synchronization, making end-to-end performance sensitive to workload imbalance. We present DWDP (Distributed Weight Data Parallelism), an inference parallelization strategy that preserves data-parallel execution while offloading MoE weights across peer GPUs and fetching missing experts on demand. By removing collective inter-rank synchronization, DWDP allows each GPU to progress independently. We further address the practical overheads of this design with two optimizations for split-weight management and asynchronous remote-weight prefetch. Implemented in TensorRT-LLM and evaluated with DeepSeek-R1 on GB200 NVL72, DWDP improves end-to-end output TPS/GPU by 8.8% at comparable TPS/user in the 20-100 TPS/user serving range under 8K input sequence length and 1K output sequence length.

</details>


### [12] [Optimization Opportunities for Cloud-Based Data Pipeline Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01954)
*Johannes Jablonski,Georg-Daniel Schwarz,Philip Heltweg,Dirk Riehle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对云数据管道基础设施优化研究进行了系统综述，提出了包含成本、执行时间等目标及多维度特征的优化理论框架，指出了多租户环境研究不足等空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云基础设施需要支持数据管道在成本、速度和资源利用率等方面的高效运行，但现有研究缺乏对优化机会的整合视角，因此需要通过系统综述来梳理优化方法并提供理论框架。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，对云基础设施数据管道性能优化的现有文献进行全面回顾与分析，整合优化目标与维度。

Result: 构建了云数据管道优化理论框架，涵盖最小化成本、减少执行时间、成本与完工时间权衡等优化目标，以及单云与多云、批处理与流处理等维度；识别出多租户环境探索不足、缺乏行业评估等研究空白。

Conclusion: 为云数据管道基础设施优化提供了系统化的理论视角，指出了当前研究的不足之处，并为未来研究方向提供了建议。

Abstract: Cloud infrastructure supports the efficient operation of data pipelines regarding requirements like cost, speed, and resource utilization. We present an integrated view of optimization opportunities for cloud-based data pipelines by conducting a systematic review of existing literature on optimization approaches to cloud infrastructure performance for data pipelines. Our study contributes a theory of optimization goals like minimizing cost, reducing execution time, and cost-makespan trade-offs, consisting of dimensions such as single vs. multi-cloud, batch vs. stream processing, etc. We highlight gaps in primary research, including the underexploration of multi-tenant environments and lack of industry evaluation, and suggest directions for future research.

</details>


### [13] [Intelligent Cloud Orchestration: A Hybrid Predictive and Heuristic Framework for Cost Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02131)
*Heet Nagoriya,Komal Rohit*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合编排框架，结合LSTM预测扩展和启发式任务分配，在降低云基础设施成本的同时保持快速响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算中动态工作负载变化常导致过度配置和成本增加。机器学习方法（如LSTM）虽能有效预测工作负载，但在流量突增时会产生延迟；而博弈论等数学启发式方法虽能快速调度，却无法考虑未来负载变化。需要解决预测准确性与响应速度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出一种混合编排框架，将基于LSTM的预测性扩展与启发式任务分配相结合。该框架利用LSTM进行工作负载模式预测，同时使用启发式方法进行快速调度决策。

Result: 该方法将基础设施成本降低至接近纯ML模型的水平，同时保持了与启发式方法相当的快速响应时间。

Conclusion: 该工作为提高云资源管理的成本效率提供了一种实用方法，成功平衡了预测准确性与响应速度的矛盾。

Abstract: Cloud computing allows scalable resource provisioning, but dynamic workload changes often lead to higher costs due to over-provisioning. Machine learning (ML) approaches, such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, are effective for predicting workload patterns at a higher level, but they can introduce delays during sudden traffic spikes. In contrast, mathematical heuristics like Game Theory provide fast and reliable scheduling decisions, but they do not account for future workload changes. To address this trade-off, this paper proposes a hybrid orchestration framework that combines LSTM-based predictive scaling with heuristic task allocation. The results show that this approach reduces infrastructure costs close to ML-based models while maintaining fast response times similar to heuristic methods. This work presents a practical approach for improving cost efficiency in cloud resource management.

</details>


### [14] [A Practical Two-Stage Framework for GPU Resource and Power Prediction in Heterogeneous HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02158)
*Beste Oztop,Dhruva Kulkarni,Zhengji Zhao,Ayse Kivilcim Coskun,Kadidia Konate*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过分析材料科学应用VASP在Perlmutter超算上的GPU资源使用数据，提出了一个两阶段的资源预测框架，仅使用Slurm日志即可实现高达97%的GPU利用率预测准确率，结合DCGM指标后功率预测准确率达92%，为HPC系统的高效调度和动态功耗管理提供了有效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算（HPC）对GPU需求的不断增长，GPU资源和功耗的高效利用变得至关重要。为了实现更高效的调度决策和功耗感知的系统运行，需要深入理解应用的实际资源使用情况并建立准确的预测模型。

Method: 利用Slurm工作负载管理器的历史日志和NVIDIA DCGM收集的GPU性能指标，对广泛使用的材料科学应用VASP进行GPU利用率、显存利用率和功耗分析。在此基础上提出两阶段资源预测框架：第一阶段仅使用Slurm记账日志作为训练数据；第二阶段结合DCGM收集的历史GPU性能指标增强训练数据。

Result: 仅使用Slurm提交特征的GPU利用率最大值预测准确率高达97%；从GPU计算和内存活动指标提取的特征与平均功耗利用率具有良好的相关性，运行时功耗使用预测实验准确率高达92%。这些结果证明了DCGM指标在捕获应用特征方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明DCGM指标能够有效捕获应用特征，在开发支持HPC系统动态功耗管理的预测模型方面具有重要潜力，可为异构HPC系统应用的调度决策和功耗感知系统运行提供有力支持。

Abstract: Efficient utilization of GPU resources and power has become critical with the growing demand for GPUs in high-performance computing (HPC). In this paper, we analyze GPU utilization and GPU memory utilization, as well as the power consumption of the Vienna ab initio Simulation Package (VASP), using the Slurm workload manager historical logs and GPU performance metrics collected by NVIDIA's Data Center GPU Manager (DCGM). VASP is a widely used materials science application on Perlmutter at NERSC, an HPE Cray EX system based on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. Using our insights from the resource utilization analysis of VASP applications, we propose a resource prediction framework to predict the average GPU power, maximum GPU utilization, and maximum GPU memory utilization values of heterogeneous HPC system applications to enable more efficient scheduling decisions and power-aware system operation. Our prediction framework consists of two stages: 1) using only the Slurm accounting logs as training data and 2) augmenting the training data with historical GPU profiling metrics collected with DCGM. The maximum GPU utilization predictions using only the Slurm submission features achieve up to 97% accuracy. Furthermore, features engineered from GPU-compute and memory activity metrics exhibit good correlations with average power utilization, and our runtime power usage prediction experiments result in up to 92% prediction accuracy. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of DCGM metrics in capturing application characteristics and highlight their potential for developing predictive models to support dynamic power management in HPC systems.

</details>


### [15] [What can be computed in average anonymous networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02192)
*Joel Rybicki,Oleg Verbitsky,Maksim Zhukovskii*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在匿名随机图上，证明弱模型SB与强模型LOCAL在计算能力上几乎等价，并给出高效的分布式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在极弱的分布式计算模型（匿名节点、广播通信）中，确定性算法在随机输入图上的计算能力，旨在理解弱模型的计算边界及其与标准模型的关系。

Method: 设计基于图同构规范标号技术的一轮确定性SB算法，使用O(log n)比特消息在G(n,p)随机图上高概率生成唯一标识符；由此构造匿名化算法，并分析随机图场景下Monte Carlo与Las Vegas类分布式算法的计算层次。

Result: 在参数满足n^{ε-1}≤p≤1/2的G(n,p)上，所提算法以高概率生成唯一标识符；应用于三角形查找可在O(1/ε)轮完成；除极小部分图外，SB模型在G(n,p)上与LOCAL模型计算能力等价，导致模型层次坍缩。

Conclusion: 极弱的SB模型在随机图环境下几乎与LOCAL模型同样强大，为匿名网络上的分布式算法设计提供了理论与算法基础。

Abstract: We study what deterministic distributed algorithms can compute on random input graphs in extremely weak models of distributed computing: all nodes are anonymous, and in each communication round, nodes broadcast a message to all their neighbors, receive a (multi)set of messages from their neighbors, and update their local state. These correspond to the SB and MB models introduced by Hella et al. [PODC 2012] and are strictly weaker than the standard port-numbering PN and LOCAL models.
  We investigate what can be computed almost surely on random input graphs. We give a one-round deterministic SB-algorithm using $O(\log n)$-bit messages that computes unique identifiers with high probability on anonymous networks sampled from $G(n,p)$, where $n^{\varepsilon-1} \le p \le 1/2$ and $\varepsilon>0$ is an arbitrarily small constant. This algorithm is inspired by canonical labeling techniques in graph isomorphism testing and can be used to "anonymize" existing distributed graph algorithms designed for the broadcast CONGEST and LOCAL models. In particular, we give a new anonymous algorithm that finds a triangle in $O(1/\varepsilon)$ rounds on the above input distribution.
  We also investigate computational power of natural analogs of "Monte Carlo" and "Las Vegas" distributed graph algorithms in the random graph setting, and establish some new collapse and hierarchy results. For example, our work shows the collapse of the weak model hierarchy of Hella et al. on $G(n,p)$, as apart from a vanishingly small fraction of input graphs, the SB model is as powerful as LOCAL.

</details>

### [16] [DAXFS: A Lock-Free Shared Filesystem for CXL Disaggregated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01620)
*Cong Wang,Yiwei Yang,Yusheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出了DaxFS，这是一个面向CXL共享内存的Linux文件系统，利用跨主机一致的cmpxchg原子操作作为唯一协调原语，实现了无锁多主机并发写入和去中心化的共享页缓存淘汰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CXL技术使多主机能够共享具有硬件缓存一致性的字节寻址内存，但现有文件系统尚未利用此特性进行无锁多主机协调。研究旨在探索如何利用CXL的跨主机原子操作原语，构建高效的多主机协调机制。

Method: 采用CAS（Compare-and-Swap）哈希叠加层实现多主机无锁并发写入，无需集中协调器；设计协作共享页缓存，并提出新颖的多主机时钟淘汰算法（MH-clock），在共享DAX内存中通过cmpxchg实现完全去中心化的受害者页面选择；使用QEMU模拟的CXL 3.0环境验证多主机正确性。

Result: 在跨主机争用下，DaxFS保持了超过99%的CAS准确率且无丢失更新；在单主机DRAM支持的DAX上，所有写入工作负载的吞吐量均超过tmpfs，4线程下随机写入吞吐量最高提升2.68倍，64 KB时随机读取吞吐量提升1.18倍；GPU微基准测试表明该设计可扩展至GPU线程以PCIe 5.0带宽限制执行页缓存操作。

Conclusion: DaxFS成功证明了利用CXL跨主机一致的原子操作构建无锁多主机文件系统的可行性，为CXL共享内存环境下的多主机协调提供了高效解决方案，具有良好的性能表现和可扩展性。

Abstract: CXL (Compute Express Link) enables multiple hosts to share byte-addressable memory with hardware cache coherence, but no existing filesystem exploits this for lock-free multi-host coordination. We present DaxFS, a Linux filesystem for CXL shared memory that uses cmpxchg atomic operations, which CXL makes coherent across host boundaries, as its sole coordination primitive. A CAS-based hash overlay enables lock-free concurrent writes from multiple hosts without any centralized coordinator. A cooperative shared page cache with a novel multi-host clock eviction algorithm (MH-clock) provides demand-paged caching in shared DAX memory, with fully decentralized victim selection via cmpxchg. We validate multi-host correctness using QEMU-emulated CXL 3.0, where two virtual hosts share a memory region with TCP-forwarded atomics. Under cross-host contention, DaxFS maintains >99% CAS accuracy with no lost updates. On single-host DRAM-backed DAX, DaxFS exceeds tmpfs throughput across all write workloads, achieving up to 2.68x higher random write throughput with 4 threads and 1.18x higher random read throughput at 64 KB. Preliminary GPU microbenchmarks show that the cmpxchg-based design extends to GPU threads performing page cache operations at PCIe 5.0 bandwidth limits.

</details>


### [17] [HACache: Leveraging Read Performance with Cache in a Heterogeneous Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01655)
*Jialin Liu,Liang Shi,Dingcui Yu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出HACache，利用高性能SSD作为读缓存解决异构RAID性能瓶颈问题，带宽提升约35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在成本敏感场景下，RAID阵列可能混合使用不同性能等级的SSD（如老化降级的设备与新设备混合）。传统条带化机制均匀分布请求，导致慢速SSD成为瓶颈，快速SSD利用率不足，整体带宽受限。

Method: 提出HACache（异构自适应缓存）：1）形式化请求分流问题并求解；2）采用两阶段请求分流比例调整机制，支持运行时最优分流比例搜索；3）引入缓存容量调控机制，根据命中率和请求分流需求为每个后端SSD自适应配额。

Result: 实验表明，HACache显著提升异构RAID读性能，在典型混合配置下带宽提升约35%。

Conclusion: HACache通过将高性能SSD用作读缓存来重新平衡请求分布，有效解决了异构SSD RAID阵列的性能瓶颈问题，最大化带宽利用率。

Abstract: In cost-sensitive deployments, RAID arrays may combine SSDs with different performance levels. Such heterogeneity arises when aging SSDs degrade yet remain usable, or when failed drives are replaced with new devices of explicitly better performance. While this reduces procurement cost, it creates performance challenges: traditional striping mecahnism distributes requests evenly, but slower SSDs become bottlenecks, leaving faster ones underutilized and limiting overall bandwidth to the slowest drive.
  To address this, we propose HACache (Heterogeneity Adaptive Cache) for read-intensive workloads. HACache introduces high-performance SSDs as read caches to rebalance request distribution. First, we formalize the request diversion problem and solve it formally. Second, to support optimal diversion ratios searching at runtime, HACache adopts a two-phase request diversion ratio adjustment mechanism. Finally, a cache capacity regulation is adopted to adapt quotas for each backend SSD based on hit rates and request diversion needs. This design maximizes bandwidth utilization. Experiments show HACache improves heterogeneous RAID read performance significantly, with bandwidth gains of about 35\% in typical mixed configurations.

</details>

### [18] [FlatAttention: Dataflow and Fabric Collectives Co-Optimization for Large Attention-Based Model Inference on Tile-Based Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02110)
*Chi Zhang,Luca Colagrande,Renzo Andri,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FlatAttention数据流，面向基于Tile架构的加速器优化MoE模型注意力计算，相比FlashAttention-3实现4.1倍加速，HBM流量降低16倍，在DeepSeek-v3上实现1.9倍系统吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合专家（MoE）模型推理中，注意力计算占据主导地位，且新兴领域专用加速器正转向芯片级和晶圆级基于瓦片的架构，其片上互联支持瓦片间通信以减少主存访问瓶颈，因此数据流管理对于实现高利用率至关重要。

Method: 提出FlatAttention数据流方案，通过利用集成在片上网络结构中的集合原语来最小化昂贵的高带宽内存（HBM）访问，优化现代注意力变体在基于瓦片加速器上的执行。

Result: 在32x32瓦片配置下实现高达92.3%的利用率，相比FlashAttention-3实现4.1倍加速和16倍更低的HBM流量；计算密集型注意力平均利用率86%，内存密集型注意力HBM带宽利用率78%，平均比GH200快1.9倍；在DeepSeek-v3 FP8解码中实现1.9倍系统吞吐量提升和1.4倍每用户token输出延迟降低。

Conclusion: FlatAttention成功在基于瓦片的架构上高效加速多种注意力变体，显著提升了系统吞吐量和延迟表现，证明了该方法在现代大规模模型推理加速中的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Attention accounts for an increasingly dominant fraction of total computation during inference for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, making efficient acceleration critical. Emerging domain-specific accelerators for large model inference are shifting toward chip-scale and wafer-scale tile-based architectures. Tiles contain large matrix and vector engines and are connected through on-chip interconnects, which support tile-to-tile traffic to reduce the tile-to-main-memory traffic bottleneck. Hence, dataflow management is crucial to achieve high utilization. We propose FlatAttention, a dataflow for modern attention variants on tile-based accelerators. FlatAttention minimizes expensive high-bandwidth memory (HBM) accesses by exploiting collective primitives integrated into the on-chip network fabric, achieving up to 92.3% utilization, 4.1x speedup over FlashAttention-3, and 16x lower HBM traffic. On a 32x32 tile configuration with peak performance comparable to NVIDIA GH200, FlatAttention generalizes across multiple attention variants, achieving an average of 86% utilization for compute-bound attentions and 78% HBM bandwidth utilization for memory-bound ones, resulting in an average 1.9x speedup over attention implementations on GH200. Finally, we evaluate end-to-end DeepSeek-v3 FP8 decoding with FlatAttention on a wafer-scale multi-die system, achieving a 1.9x improvement in system throughput and a 1.4x reduction in per-user token output latency, despite operating with 1.5x lower peak system performance compared to the state-of-the-art solution.

</details>


### [19] [GEMM-GS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting on Tensor Cores with GEMM-Compatible Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02120)
*Haomin Li,Bowen Zhu,Fangxin Liu,Zongwu Wang,Xinran Liang,Li Jiang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GEMM-GS通过将3DGS的混合过程转换为GEMM兼容形式，利用GPU Tensor Cores实现加速，相比原始3DGS获得1.42倍加速，与现有加速方法结合可获得额外1.47倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)虽然相比NeRF有所改进，但仍无法满足实际实时需求。现有的加速工作忽视了现代GPU中Tensor Cores的演进，因为3DGS流程缺乏通用矩阵乘法(GEMM)操作，无法有效利用Tensor Cores的计算能力。

Method: 提出GEMM-GS加速方法，通过GEMM友好的混合变换，将3DGS混合过程等价转换为GEMM兼容形式，从而能够利用GPU上的Tensor Cores。设计了高性能CUDA内核，集成了三级双缓冲流水线，实现计算和内存访问的重叠。

Result: 实验结果表明，GEMM-GS相比原始3DGS实现1.42倍加速，与现有加速方法结合时平均可获得额外1.47倍加速。

Conclusion: GEMM-GS成功地将3DGS流程转换为能够利用Tensor Cores的形式，有效提升了3DGS的渲染性能，证明了通过算法变换来适应现代GPU硬件特性的有效性。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) enables 3D scene reconstruction from several 2D images but incurs high rendering latency via its point-sampling design. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) improves on NeRF with explicit scene representation and an optimized pipeline yet still fails to meet practical real-time demands. Existing acceleration works overlook the evolving Tensor Cores of modern GPUs because 3DGS pipeline lacks General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM) operations. This paper proposes GEMM-GS, an acceleration approach utilizing tensor cores on GPUs via GEMM-friendly blending transformation. It equivalently reformulates the 3DGS blending process into a GEMM-compatible form to utilize Tensor Cores. A high-performance CUDA kernel is designed, integrating a three-stage double-buffered pipeline that overlaps computation and memory access. Extensive experiments show that GEMM-GS achieves $1.42\times$ speedup over vanilla 3DGS and provides an additional $1.47\times$ speedup on average when combining with existing acceleration approaches. Code is released at https://github.com/shieldforever/GEMM-GS.

</details>


### [20] [TensorPool: A 3D-Stacked 8.4TFLOPS/4.3W Many-Core Domain-Specific Processor for AI-Native Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02291)
*Marco Bertuletti,Yichao Zhang,Diyou Shen,Alessandro Vanelli-Coralli,Frank K. Gürkaynak,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出TensorPool：面向6G AI原生物理层的256核RISC-V集群，通过张量加速实现6倍性能提升和9.1倍能效改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G无线接入网中AI与物理层深度融合带来更高计算复杂度，而亚毫秒级实时约束、太赫兹载波下基站功耗限制（≤100W）对AI-Native PHY部署提出严峻挑战，亟需高能效、可编程的专用计算架构。

Method: 采用领域专用化设计，构建包含256个RISC-V32IMAF可编程核心的集群，集成16个256 MACs/周期的FP16张量引擎，配备4MiB L1暂存存储器实现最大数据复用；采用TSMC N7工艺实现，并探索3D堆叠优化版图。

Result: 在AI-RAN张量运算上达到3643 MACs/周期（张量单元利用率89%），相比纯核心集群性能提升6倍，GOPS/W/mm²效率改进9.1倍；3D堆叠实现2.32倍面积缩减且无频率损失。

Conclusion: TensorPool通过领域专用化的多核可编程架构与张量加速，有效解决了AI-Native PHY在高实时性、低功耗约束下的部署难题，为6G基站提供了高性能、高能效的计算平台。

Abstract: The upcoming integration of AI in the physical layer (PHY) of 6G radio access networks (RAN) will enable a higher quality of service in challenging transmission scenarios. However, deeply optimized AI-Native PHY models impose higher computational complexity compared to conventional baseband, challenging deployment under the sub-msec real-time constraints typical of modern PHYs. Additionally, following the extension to terahertz carriers, the upcoming densification of 6G cell-sites further limits the power consumption of base stations, constraining the budget available for compute ($\leq$ 100W). The desired flexibility to ensure long term sustainability and the imperative energy-efficiency gains on the high-throughput tensor computations dominating AI-Native PHYs can be achieved by domain-specialization of many-core programmable baseband processors. Following the domain-specialization strategy, we present TensorPool, a cluster of 256 RISCV32IMAF programmable cores, accelerated by 16 256 MACs/cycle (FP16) tensor engines with low-latency access to 4MiB of L1 scratchpad for maximal data-reuse. Implemented in TSMC's N7, TensorPool achieves 3643~MACs/cycle (89% tensor-unit utilization) on tensor operations for AI-RAN, 6$\times$ more than a core-only cluster without tensor acceleration, while simultaneously improving GOPS/W/mm$^2$ efficiency by 9.1$\times$. Further, we show that 3D-stacking the computing blocks of TensorPool to better unfold the tensor engines to L1-memory routing provides 2.32$\times$ footprint improvement with no frequency degradation, compared to a 2D implementation.

</details>

### [21] [Runtime Burden Allocation for Structured LLM Routing in Agentic Expert Systems: A Full-Factorial Cross-Backend Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01235)
*Zhou Hanlin,Chan Huah Yong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 结构化LLM路由是一个系统级负载分配问题，而非单纯的提示工程问题。研究通过大规模基准测试发现不存在通用最佳路由模式，性能受后端交互效应主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型成为智能体AI系统的核心控制组件，可靠的结构化路由需要在实际部署约束下平衡正确性、延迟和实现成本。作者认为结构化路由不应仅被视为提示工程问题，而是更根本的系统级负载分配问题。

Method: 采用全因子基准测试方法，覆盖48种部署配置和15,552个请求，在OpenAI、Gemini和Llama三种后端上进行实验。研究分析结构化工作如何在生成栈中分配：模型直接输出结构、传输中压缩、或生成后本地重建。

Result: 核心发现是不存在通用最佳路由模式。后端特定的交互效应主导性能表现：在Gemini和OpenAI上高度可靠的模式在Llama上可能出现显著正确性下降，压缩实现的效率增益强烈依赖于后端类型。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可部署的框架，用于在异构后端条件下推理结构化路由。提供了跨后端评估方法论和生产级智能体专家系统中导航正确性-成本-延迟前沿的实用部署指导。

Abstract: Structured LLM routing is often treated as a prompt-engineering problem. We argue that it is, more fundamentally, a systems-level burden-allocation problem. As large language models (LLMs) become core control components in agentic AI systems, reliable structured routing must balance correctness, latency, and implementation cost under real deployment constraints. We show that this balance is shaped not only by prompts or schemas, but also by how structural work is allocated across the generation stack: whether output structure is emitted directly by the model, compressed during transport, or reconstructed locally after generation.
  We evaluate this formulation through a comprehensive full-factorial benchmark covering 48 deployment configurations and 15,552 requests across OpenAI, Gemini, and Llama backends. Our central finding is consequential: there is no universal best routing mode. Instead, backend-specific interaction effects dominate performance. Modes that remain highly reliable on Gemini and OpenAI can suffer substantial correctness degradation on Llama, while efficiency gains from compressed realization are strongly backend-dependent.
  Rather than presenting another isolated model comparison, this work contributes a deployable framework for reasoning about structured routing under heterogeneous backend conditions. We provide a cross-backend evaluation methodology and practical deployment guidance for navigating the correctness-cost-latency frontier in production-grade agentic expert systems.

</details>


### [22] [The Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework: A Validation Architecture for Simulated Potential Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01325)
*Olav Laudy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出数字孪生反事实框架（DTCF），通过仿真模拟而非统计估计来生成反事实，并建立五级验证架构将不可证伪的声明转化为可检验的测试，形式化区分了边际验证的因果量与依赖copula的联合因果量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断的根本问题在于个体层面的反事实结果永远无法观测。现有方法（如可忽略性、平行趋势、排他性约束）都用假设替代缺失数据，但都无法真正产生反事实本身。研究者需要一个框架，能够模拟反事实并通过验证使其主张可被检验。

Method: 1. 将数字孪生仿真器形式化为潜在结果框架内的随机映射；2. 引入三个层级的孪生保真度假设（边际保真、联合保真、结构保真），每层解锁更丰富的可估计量类别；3. 构建五级验证架构，将仿真器产生正确反事实的不可证伪主张转化为针对可观测数据的可证伪测试；4. 建立形式化分解，区分边际可验证因果量与copula依赖因果量；5. 开发边界分析、敏感性分析和不确定性量化工具。

Result: 1. 边际验证的因果量（ATE、CATE、QTE）可通过可观测臂比较进行测试；2. copula依赖的因果量（ITE分布、受益/受损概率、处理效应方差）永久依赖不可观测的个体内依赖结构；3. 提供的工具使copula依赖关系显式化；4. 边际因果主张变得日益可测试，联合因果主张变得显式地以假设为索引，两者之间的差距得到形式化刻画。

Conclusion: DTCF并未解决因果推断的根本问题，但提供了一个框架：边际因果主张变得日益可测试，联合因果主张变得显式地以假设为索引，两者之间的差距被形式化表征。该框架将不可证伪的声明转化为可检验的测试，为因果推断实践提供了更严格的方法论基础。

Abstract: The fundamental problem of causal inference - that the counterfactual outcome for any individual is never observed - has shaped the entire methodology of the field. Every existing approach substitutes assumptions for missing data: ignorability, parallel trends, exclusion restrictions. None produces the counterfactual itself. This paper proposes the Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework (DTCF): rather than estimating the counterfactual statistically, we simulate it using a digital twin and subject the simulation to a hierarchical validation regime. We formalize the digital twin simulator as a stochastic mapping within the potential outcomes framework and introduce a hierarchy of twin fidelity assumptions - from marginal fidelity through joint fidelity to structural fidelity - each unlocking a progressively richer class of estimands. The central contribution is threefold. First, a five-level validation architecture converts the unfalsifiable claim that the simulator produces correct counterfactuals into falsifiable tests against observable data. Second, a formal decomposition separates causal quantities into those that are marginally validated (ATE, CATE, QTE - testable through observable-arm comparison) and those that are copula-dependent (the ITE distribution, probability of benefit/harm, variance of treatment effects - permanently reliant on the unobservable within-individual dependence structure). Third, bounding, sensitivity, and uncertainty quantification tools make the copula dependence explicit. The DTCF does not resolve the fundamental problem of causal inference. What it provides is a framework in which marginal causal claims become increasingly testable, joint causal claims become explicitly assumption-indexed, and the gap between the two is formally characterized.

</details>


### [23] [IDEA2: Expert-in-the-loop competency question elicitation for collaborative ontology engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01344)
*Elliott Watkiss-Leek,Reham Alharbi,Harry Rostron,Andrew Ng,Ewan Johnson,Andrew Mitchell,Terry R. Payne,Valentina Tamma,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IDEA2，一种半自动化工作流，利用大语言模型（LLM）和专家在环协作，解决本体工程中能力问题（CQ）提取的资源密集型瓶颈，并通过两个实际场景验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力问题（CQ）提取是本体工程中的关键但资源密集型环节，常因领域专家与本体工程师之间的沟通鸿沟而受阻。需要一种方法来弥合这一鸿沟，提高CQ提取的效率和质量。

Method: 提出IDEA2半自动化工作流，采用核心迭代循环：(1) 基于LLM从需求文档中初始提取CQ；(2) 领域专家在协作平台上进行共创性审查和反馈；(3) LLM根据反馈迭代重述被拒绝的CQ直至达成共识。使用溯源模型追踪每个CQ的完整生命周期。

Result: 在科学数据和文化遗产两个真实场景中验证，IDEA2能够加速需求工程过程，提高所生成CQ的接受度和相关性，并在领域专家中展现出高可用性和有效性。

Conclusion: IDEA2成功整合了LLM与专家协作，有效解决了本体工程中CQ提取的瓶颈问题，为半自动化本体工程提供了可行方案，代码和实验已开源。

Abstract: Competency question (CQ) elicitation represents a critical but resource-intensive bottleneck in ontology engineering. This foundational phase is often hampered by the communication gap between domain experts, who possess the necessary knowledge, and ontology engineers, who formalise it. This paper introduces IDEA2, a novel, semi-automated workflow that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) within a collaborative, expert-in-the-loop process to address this challenge. The methodology is characterised by a core iterative loop: an initial LLM-based extraction of CQs from requirement documents, a co-creational review and feedback phase by domain experts on an accessible collaborative platform, and an iterative, feedback-driven reformulation of rejected CQs by an LLM until consensus is achieved. To ensure transparency and reproducibility, the entire lifecycle of each CQ is tracked using a provenance model that captures the full lineage of edits, anonymised feedback, and generation parameters. The workflow was validated in 2 real-world scenarios (scientific data, cultural heritage), demonstrating that IDEA2 can accelerate the requirements engineering process, improve the acceptance and relevance of the resulting CQs, and exhibit high usability and effectiveness among domain experts. We release all code and experiments at https://github.com/KE-UniLiv/IDEA2

</details>


### [24] [A Role-Based LLM Framework for Structured Information Extraction from Healthy Food Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01529)
*Congjing Zhang,Ruoxuan Bao,Jingyu Li,Yoav Ackerman,Shuai Huang,Yanfang Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于角色的LLM框架，通过模拟专家分析工作流程，成功解决了健康食品政策领域信息提取中的幻觉、误分类和遗漏问题，在复杂推理任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在健康食品政策领域的信息提取任务中面临诸多挑战，由于政策文档的结构多样性和不一致性，导致模型产生幻觉、误分类和遗漏等错误信息，缺乏可靠性。

Method: 提出了一种基于角色的LLM框架，将领域专家角色分配给不同的LLM：政策分析师负责元数据和机制分类、法律策略专家识别复杂法律方法、食品系统专家对食品系统阶段进行分类。该框架通过在角色特定的提示中融入结构化领域知识（包括法律机制的明确定义和分类标准），模拟专家分析工作流程。使用Llama-3.3-70B在608个健康食品政策数据集上进行评估，与零样本、少样本和思维链基线方法进行对比。

Result: 所提出的框架在复杂推理任务中表现出优越性能，相比零样本、少样本和思维链等基线方法具有显著优势，为健康政策信息提取自动化提供了可靠且透明的方法。

Conclusion: 基于角色的LLM框架能够有效解决健康食品政策信息提取中的关键问题，通过模拟专家工作流程和融入领域知识，实现了更可靠、透明的自动化信息提取，为政策分析领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Current Large Language Model (LLM) approaches for information extraction (IE) in the healthy food policy domain are often hindered by various factors, including misinformation, specifically hallucinations, misclassifications, and omissions that result from the structural diversity and inconsistency of policy documents. To address these limitations, this study proposes a role-based LLM framework that automates the IE from unstructured policy data by assigning specialized roles: an LLM policy analyst for metadata and mechanism classification, an LLM legal strategy specialist for identifying complex legal approaches, and an LLM food system expert for categorizing food system stages. This framework mimics expert analysis workflows by incorporating structured domain knowledge, including explicit definitions of legal mechanisms and classification criteria, into role-specific prompts. We evaluate the framework using 608 healthy food policies from the Healthy Food Policy Project (HFPP) database, comparing its performance against zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought (CoT) baselines using Llama-3.3-70B. Our proposed framework demonstrates superior performance in complex reasoning tasks, offering a reliable and transparent methodology for automating IE from health policies.

</details>


### [25] [Semantic Modeling for World-Centered Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01359)
*Andrei Mantsivoda,Darya Gavrilina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以世界为中心的多智能体系统（WMAS）架构，通过共享显式的世界表征解决传统以智能体为中心架构在结构化领域中的语义一致性和可解释性问题，并给出数学形式化和平台实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统以智能体为中心的架构在企业和制度系统等结构化领域中存在语义不一致、缺乏可解释性和长期稳定性不足的问题，需要一种新的系统架构来确保全局一致性和可验证的系统行为。

Method: 提出以世界为中心的多智能体系统（WMAS）架构，从本体显式性、规范性等维度对世界进行分类；采用语义模型作为表示世界的数学形式化方法；学习与协调在共享世界模型上操作，而非隔离的智能体局部表征；开发Ontobox平台作为WMAS的实现。

Result: 建立了WMAS的理论框架，实现了学习与协调在共享世界模型上的操作，能够确保全局一致性、语义一致性、可解释性和可验证的系统行为，并通过Ontobox平台验证了架构的可行性。

Conclusion: 以世界为中心的多智能体系统架构为结构化领域提供了一种可行的替代方案，通过共享显式的世界表征解决了传统架构的关键问题，为企业级和制度系统应用提供了理论基础和实践平台。

Abstract: We introduce world-centered multi-agent systems (WMAS) as an alternative to traditional agent-centered architectures, arguing that structured domains such as enterprises and institutional systems require a shared, explicit world representation to ensure semantic consistency, explainability, and long-term stability. We classify worlds along dimensions including ontological explicitness, normativity, etc. In WMAS, learning and coordination operate over a shared world model rather than isolated agent-local representations, enabling global consistency and verifiable system behavior. We propose semantic models as a mathematical formalism for representing such worlds. Finally, we present the Ontobox platform as a realization of WMAS.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 比较了各类控制器的容量区域和平均行程时间（ATT）性能；参数共享控制器成功泛化到更大规模网络；发现即使路口间没有显式协调，交通流也可能自组织形成"绿波"现象。

Conclusion: 强化学习控制器在多路口交通网络中具有应用潜力，参数共享策略具有良好的泛化能力，即使缺乏正式协调机制，系统也可能展现出有益的自组织特性。

Abstract: In this work, we extend our systematic capacity region perspective to multi-junction traffic networks, focussing on the special case of an urban corridor network. In particular, we train and evaluate centralized, fully decentralized, and parameter-sharing decentralized RL controllers, and compare their capacity regions and ATTs together with a classical baseline MaxPressure controller. Further, we show how the parametersharing controller may be generalised to be deployed on a larger network than it was originally trained on. In this setting, we show some initial findings that suggest that even though the junctions are not formally coordinated, traffic may self organise into `green waves'.

</details>


### [27] [Crashing Waves vs. Rising Tides: Preliminary Findings on AI Automation from Thousands of Worker Evaluations of Labor Market Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01363)
*Matthias Mertens,Adam Kuzee,Brittany S. Harris,Harry Lyu,Wensu Li,Jonathan Rosenfeld,Meiri Anto,Martin Fleming,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现AI自动化主要表现为“上涨潮汐”（广泛、连续的能力提升），而非“破碎波浪”（少数任务的突然飞跃）。基于对3000多项文本任务的评估，AI在2024年中期可约50%成功率完成人类需3-4小时的任务，预计到2029年能在大多数文本任务上达到80%-95%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI自动化的演进模式是呈现为少数任务上能力突然大幅跃升的“破碎波浪”，还是表现为广泛任务上能力持续、渐进提升的“上涨潮汐”，以理解AI对劳动力市场影响的节奏与范围。

Method: 基于美国劳工部O*NET分类，构建了3000多项适用于大语言模型（LLM）的文本任务数据集；通过相关工作领域的工人进行了超过17,000次评估，以此测试并量化AI在不同任务上的能力表现及其随时间的演变趋势。

Result: 几乎没有证据支持“破碎波浪”效应，但发现了支持“上涨潮汐”效应的有力证据。数据显示，AI在广泛任务上表现优异且提升迅速：2024年第二季度，AI能以约50%的成功率完成人类需3-4小时的任务，预计到2025年第三季度成功率将升至约65%。若趋势持续，到2029年LLM在大多数文本任务上的平均成功率将达到80%-95%。要达到近乎完美的成功率或更高质量水平则需要额外数年时间。

Conclusion: AI能力提升主要呈现为广泛、连续的“上涨潮汐”模式。这一趋势意味着AI将对经济和劳动力市场产生深远影响，但组织层面的AI采用及其带来的广泛经济影响，其时间线可能会比技术能力本身的提升更长。

Abstract: We propose that AI automation is a continuum between: (i) crashing waves where AI capabilities surge abruptly over small sets of tasks, and (ii) rising tides where the increase in AI capabilities is more continuous and broad-based. We test for these effects in preliminary evidence from an ongoing evaluation of AI capabilities across over 3,000 broad-based tasks derived from the U.S. Department of Labor O*NET categorization that are text-based and thus LLM-addressable. Based on more than 17,000 evaluations by workers from these jobs, we find little evidence of crashing waves (in contrast to recent work by METR), but substantial evidence that rising tides are the primary form of AI automation. AI performance is high and improving rapidly across a wide range of tasks. We estimate that, in 2024-Q2, AI models successfully complete tasks that take humans approximately 3-4 hours with about a 50% success rate, increasing to about 65% by 2025-Q3. If recent trends in AI capability growth persist, this pace of AI improvement implies that LLMs will be able to complete most text-related tasks with success rates of, on average, 80%-95% by 2029 at a minimally sufficient quality level. Achieving near-perfect success rates at this quality level or comparable success rates at superior quality would require several additional years. These AI capability improvements would impact the economy and labor market as organizations adopt AI, which could have a substantially longer timeline.

</details>

*Andrew Ang,Nazym Azimbayev,Andrey Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于代理AI的战略资产配置流程，利用约50个专业智能体生成资本市场假设、构建投资组合，并通过自我批判和投票机制进行决策，同时由投资政策声明(IPS)进行整体治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理AI技术的发展，投资者角色正从分析执行转向监督 oversight。研究旨在探索如何将代理AI应用于战略资产配置领域，实现从人工执行到AI自主决策的转变，同时保持适当的治理框架。

Method: 构建了一个包含约50个专业化智能体的资产配置流程：(1)智能体生成资本市场假设；(2)使用20多种竞争性方法构建投资组合；(3)智能体之间相互批判和投票；(4)研究者智能体提出新的组合构建方法；(5)元智能体对比历史预测与实际收益，重写智能体代码和提示词以优化性能；(6)整个流程由投资政策声明(IPS)治理约束。

Result: 成功实现了代理AI在战略资产配置中的应用，形成了一个完整的多智能体协作系统。该系统能够自主执行专业投资分析任务，具备自我改进能力（通过元智能体），并且可以被现有的投资治理文件（IPS）所约束和指导。

Conclusion: 代理AI能够有效改变投资者角色，将人类从分析执行者转变为监督者。与人工组合管理者使用相同的治理文档（IPS）可以约束和指导自主智能体，为AI驱动的投资管理提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Agentic AI shifts the investor's role from analytical execution to oversight. We present an agentic strategic asset allocation pipeline in which approximately 50 specialized agents produce capital market assumptions, construct portfolios using over 20 competing methods, and critique and vote on each other's output. A researcher agent proposes new portfolio construction methods not yet represented, and a meta-agent compares past forecasts against realized returns and rewrites agent code and prompts to improve future performance. The entire pipeline is governed by the Investment Policy Statement--the same document that guides human portfolio managers can now constrain and direct autonomous agents.

</details>


### [29] [CogBias: Measuring and Mitigating Cognitive Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01366)
*Fan Huang,Songheng Zhang,Haewoon Kwak,Jisun An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了LLM CogBias基准，发现大语言模型在四类认知偏差上表现出系统性行为，证实这些偏差在模型激活空间中呈线性可分方向编码，通过激活引导可实现26-32%的偏差降低且保持下游能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明LLMs在行为上表现出认知偏差，但这些偏差是否对应可识别的内部表示、能否通过针对性干预进行缓解仍是未解之谜。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究认知偏差的内部机制及其可干预性。

Method: 定义了LLM认知偏差并引入LLM CogBias基准（包含判断、信息处理、社会和响应四类偏差家族）；采用对比设计下的线性探针分析偏差在激活空间中的编码方式；应用激活引导技术对偏差行为进行调控。

Result: 认知偏差在四类家族中系统性出现，提示级去偏对响应类偏差显著有效但对判断类偏差产生反效果；偏差在激活空间中编码为线性可分方向；激活引导实现26-32%的偏差降低，Llama模型下游能力几乎无损，Qwen在判断偏差上最多下降19.0pp；跨模型偏差表示近正交（余弦相似度0.01），但引导效果相似（r(246)=.621）。

Conclusion: LLMs中的认知偏差可作为激活空间中的线性方向被识别和调控，激活引导是有效的去偏方法且在保持模型能力的同时跨架构具有一致性，揭示了LLMs共享的功能组织结构。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making contexts. While prior work has shown that LLMs exhibit cognitive biases behaviorally, whether these biases correspond to identifiable internal representations and can be mitigated through targeted intervention remains an open question. We define LLM cognitive bias as systematic, reproducible deviations from correct answers in tasks with computable ground-truth baselines, and introduce LLM CogBias, a benchmark organized around four families of cognitive biases: Judgment, Information Processing, Social, and Response. We evaluate three LLMs and find that cognitive biases emerge systematically across all four families, with magnitudes and debiasing responses that are strongly family-dependent: prompt-level debiasing substantially reduces Response biases but backfires for Judgment biases. Using linear probes under a contrastive design, we show that these biases are encoded as linearly separable directions in model activation space. Finally, we apply activation steering to modulate biased behavior, achieving 26--32\% reduction in bias score (fraction of biased responses) while preserving downstream capability on 25 benchmarks (Llama: negligible degradation; Qwen: up to $-$19.0pp for Judgment biases). Despite near-orthogonal bias representations across models (mean cosine similarity 0.01), steering reduces bias at similar rates across architectures ($r(246)$=.621, $p$<.001), suggesting shared functional organization.

</details>


### [30] [RIFT: A RubrIc Failure Mode Taxonomy and Automated Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01375)
*Zhengyang Qi,Charles Dickens,Derek Pham,Amanda Dsouza,Armin Parchami,Frederic Sala,Paroma Varma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RIFT（RubrIc Failure mode Taxonomy），一个用于系统识别和分类LLM评分标准缺陷的框架，并开发了自动化诊断指标来替代人工评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评分标准评估方法仅依赖下游任务信号（如强化学习结果），无法从这些聚合信号中直接诊断出评分标准本身的质量问题。因此，需要一个系统性的方法来识别和分类评分标准设计中的具体失败模式。

Method: 1. 基于扎根理论，通过迭代标注五个涵盖通用指令遵循、代码生成、创意写作和深度研究等领域的基准测试中的评分标准，构建了RIFT分类法。
2. RIFT包含三类共八种失败模式：可靠性失败、内容效度失败和后果效度失败。
3. 提出了自动化的评分标准质量指标，并验证其与人工标注的一致性。

Result: 1. 构建了包含8种失败模式的RIFT分类法。
2. 人工标注一致性验证显示总体配对一致性为87%，平均Cohen's kappa为0.64，属于公平一致性水平。
3. 提出的自动化质量指标与人工失败模式标注高度对齐，F1分数最高达到0.86。

Conclusion: RIFT为诊断LLM评分标准质量提供了一个系统性的分类框架，支持从可靠性、内容效度和后果效度三个维度识别失败模式。结合提出的自动化指标，该方法能够实现可扩展的评分标准质量诊断，有效弥补了现有方法无法进行细粒度诊断的不足。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used in LLM benchmarks and training pipelines for open-ended, less verifiable tasks. While prior work has demonstrated the effectiveness of rubrics using downstream signals such as reinforcement learning outcomes, there remains no principled way to diagnose rubric quality issues from such aggregated or downstream signals alone. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT: RubrIc Failure mode Taxonomy, a taxonomy for systematically characterizing failure modes in rubric composition and design. RIFT consists of eight failure modes organized into three high-level categories: Reliability Failures, Content Validity Failures, and Consequential Validity Failures. RIFT is developed using grounded theory by iteratively annotating rubrics drawn from five diverse benchmarks spanning general instruction following, code generation, creative writing, and expert-level deep research, until no new failure modes are identified. We evaluate the consistency of the taxonomy by measuring agreement among independent human annotators, observing fair agreement overall (87% pairwise agreement and 0.64 average Cohen's kappa). Finally, to support scalable diagnosis, we propose automated rubric quality metrics and show that they align with human failure-mode annotations, achieving up to 0.86 F1.

</details>


### [31] [Leveraging the Value of Information in POMDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01434)
*Zakariya Laouar,Qi Heng Ho,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信息价值（VOI）的部分可观测蒙特卡洛规划算法（VOIMCP），通过在信息价值较低时有选择地忽略观测信息来减少分支，从而在有限规划时间内更高效地求解大规模POMDP问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管POMDP为状态和转移不确定性下的规划提供了原则性框架，但在有限规划时间内获得高性能策略仍然是一个重大挑战，主要由于维度灾难和历史灾难。针对许多POMDP问题中信息价值（VOI）在信念空间中变化的特性，需要开发能够利用这一结构的方法。

Method: 提出了一个动态规划框架，基于每个信念点的信息价值条件性地处理观测信息。在此基础上，提出了信息价值蒙特卡洛规划算法（VOIMCP），这是一种蒙特卡洛树搜索算法，当VOI较低时选择性忽略观测信息，避免不必要的观测分支，从而更有效地分配计算资源。

Result: 提供了VOI推理框架近似最优性的理论保证，并推导了VOIMCP的非渐近收敛界限。在多个POMDP基准测试上的仿真评估表明，VOIMCP优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用信息价值在信念空间中的变化结构，VOIMCP算法能够更高效地分配计算资源，在有限规划时间内获得更好的性能，为大规模POMDP问题的求解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) offer a principled formalism for planning under state and transition uncertainty. Despite advances made towards solving large POMDPs, obtaining performant policies under limited planning time remains a major challenge due to the curse of dimensionality and the curse of history. For many POMDP problems, the value of information (VOI) - the expected performance gain from reasoning about observations - varies over the belief space. We introduce a dynamic programming framework that exploits this structure by conditionally processing observations based on the value of information at each belief. Building on this framework, we propose Value of Information Monte Carlo planning (VOIMCP), a Monte Carlo Tree Search algorithm that allocates computational effort more efficiently by selectively disregarding observation information when the VOI is low, avoiding unnecessary branching of observations. We provide theoretical guarantees on the near-optimality of our VOI reasoning framework and derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds for VOIMCP. Simulation evaluations demonstrate that VOIMCP outperforms baselines on several POMDP benchmarks.

</details>


### [32] [ClawSafety: "Safe" LLMs, Unsafe Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01438)
*Bowen Wei,Yunbei Zhang,Jinhao Pan,Kai Mei,Xiao Wang,Jihun Hamm,Ziwei Zhu,Yingqiang Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLAWSAFETY基准，用于评估具有高权限的个人AI代理在面对多渠道提示注入攻击时的安全性，发现攻击成功率高达40%-75%，且安全性取决于模型与框架部署栈的共同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全评估主要在隔离的聊天环境中测试模型，依赖合成环境，且未考虑代理框架本身对安全结果的影响，无法有效评估具有高权限的个人AI代理面临的提示注入威胁（如凭证泄露、金融交易重定向或文件破坏）。

Method: 引入CLAWSAFETY基准，包含120个对抗性测试场景，沿三个维度（危害领域、攻击向量、有害动作类型）组织，基于软件工程、金融、医疗、法律和DevOps等高权限专业工作空间。测试用例将对抗性内容嵌入代理正常工作中遇到的三个渠道之一：工作空间技能文件、来自可信发送者的电子邮件和网页。评估了五个前沿LLM作为代理骨干，在所有配置中运行了2,520个沙箱试验，并在三个代理框架上进行了跨脚手架实验。

Result: 攻击成功率（ASR）在各模型中范围为40%至75%，且随注入向量不同而急剧变化，技能指令（最高信任度）始终比电子邮件或网页内容更危险。动作追踪分析显示，最强模型在凭证转发和破坏性操作上保持了硬边界，而较弱模型则允许这两种操作。跨脚手架实验进一步证明安全性不仅取决于骨干模型，还取决于完整的部署栈。

Conclusion: 安全性并非仅由骨干模型决定，而是依赖于完整的部署栈，因此需要将模型和框架视为联合变量进行安全评估。这为未来AI代理的安全设计和评估提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Personal AI agents like OpenClaw run with elevated privileges on users' local machines, where a single successful prompt injection can leak credentials, redirect financial transactions, or destroy files. This threat goes well beyond conventional text-level jailbreaks, yet existing safety evaluations fall short: most test models in isolated chat settings, rely on synthetic environments, and do not account for how the agent framework itself shapes safety outcomes. We introduce CLAWSAFETY, a benchmark of 120 adversarial test scenarios organized along three dimensions (harm domain, attack vector, and harmful action type) and grounded in realistic, high-privilege professional workspaces spanning software engineering, finance, healthcare, law, and DevOps. Each test case embeds adversarial content in one of three channels the agent encounters during normal work: workspace skill files, emails from trusted senders, and web pages. We evaluate five frontier LLMs as agent backbones, running 2,520 sandboxed trials across all configurations. Attack success rates (ASR) range from 40\% to 75\% across models and vary sharply by injection vector, with skill instructions (highest trust) consistently more dangerous than email or web content. Action-trace analysis reveals that the strongest model maintains hard boundaries against credential forwarding and destructive actions, while weaker models permit both. Cross-scaffold experiments on three agent frameworks further demonstrate that safety is not determined by the backbone model alone but depends on the full deployment stack, calling for safety evaluation that treats model and framework as joint variables.

</details>


### [33] [When AI Gets it Wong: Reliability and Risk in AI-Assisted Medication Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01449)
*Khalid Adnan Alsayed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了医疗用药AI系统的可靠性，通过模拟场景分析系统错误类型及其临床后果，发现AI错误可能导致药物不良反应或治疗延误，强调了在安全关键领域需采用风险感知的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI系统在医疗和药房工作流程（如药物推荐、剂量确定和药物相互作用检测）中表现出色，但其在现实决策中的可靠性仍不明确。在高风险的药物管理领域，单个错误建议可能导致严重患者伤害，因此需要超越传统聚合指标，关注系统失败行为及其实际影响。

Method: 通过一系列受控的模拟场景，涉及药物相互作用和剂量决策，分析不同类型的系统失败，包括遗漏的药物相互作用、错误的风险标记以及不当的剂量建议。研究重点关注错误如何发生以及AI系统产生错误输出时的后果。

Result: 研究发现，在药物相关场景中，AI错误可能导致药物不良反应、治疗无效或护理延迟，特别是在缺乏足够人工监督的情况下使用系统时。研究还揭示了过度依赖AI建议的风险以及决策过程中透明度有限带来的挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了以可靠性为核心的医疗AI评估视角，强调理解失败行为和现实影响的重要性。研究表明需要在传统性能指标基础上补充风险感知的评估方法，特别是在药房实践等安全关键领域，以确保AI系统的安全应用。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly integrated into healthcare and pharmacy workflows, supporting tasks such as medication recommendations, dosage determination, and drug interaction detection. While these systems often demonstrate strong performance under standard evaluation metrics, their reliability in real-world decision-making remains insufficiently understood. In high-risk domains such as medication management, even a single incorrect recommendation can result in severe patient harm. This paper examines the reliability of AI-assisted medication systems by focusing on system failures and their potential clinical consequences. Rather than evaluating performance solely through aggregate metrics, this work shifts attention towards how errors occur and what happens when AI systems produce incorrect outputs. Through a series of controlled, simulated scenarios involving drug interactions and dosage decisions, we analyse different types of system failures, including missed interactions, incorrect risk flagging, and inappropriate dosage recommendations. The findings highlight that AI errors in medication-related contexts can lead to adverse drug reactions, ineffective treatment, or delayed care, particularly when systems are used without sufficient human oversight. Furthermore, the paper discusses the risks of over-reliance on AI recommendations and the challenges posed by limited transparency in decision-making processes. This work contributes a reliability-focused perspective on AI evaluation in healthcare, emphasising the importance of understanding failure behavior and real-world impact. It highlights the need to complement traditional performance metrics with risk-aware evaluation approaches, particularly in safety-critical domains such as pharmacy practice.

</details>

Abstract: Scientific discovery is slowed by fragmented literature that requires excessive human effort to gather, analyze, and understand. AI tools, including autonomous summarization and question answering, have been developed to aid in understanding scientific literature. However, these tools lack the structured, multi-step approach necessary for extracting deep insights from scientific literature. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for literature analysis, but remain unreliable due to hallucinations and incomplete extraction. We introduce Elhuyar, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop system that integrates LLMs, structured AI, and human scientists to extract, analyze, and iteratively refine insights from scientific literature. The framework distributes tasks among specialized agents for filtering papers, extracting data, fitting models, and summarizing findings, with human oversight ensuring reliability. The system generates structured reports with extracted data, visualizations, model equations, and text summaries, enabling deeper inquiry through iterative refinement. Deployed in materials science, it analyzed literature on tungsten under helium-ion irradiation, showing experimentally correlated exponential helium bubble growth with irradiation dose and temperature, offering insight for plasma-facing materials (PFMs) in fusion reactors. This demonstrates how AI-assisted literature review can uncover scientific patterns and accelerate discovery.

### [35] [Infeasibility Aware Large Language Models for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01455)
*Yakun Wang,Min Chen,Zeguan Wu,Junyu Liu,Sitao Zhang,Zhenwen Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个感知不可行性的框架，结合可认证数据集构建、监督微调和LLM辅助下游搜索。通过实验证明，微调后的模型准确率相比GPT-5.2提升高达30%，同时LLM引导的热启动为下游局部搜索提供了高达2倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在解决NP难组合优化问题时，主要关注可行实例的解生成，而忽略了不可行性检测这一重要问题。大多数方法没有明确处理不可行实例的识别，这限制了LLM在实际优化问题中的应用范围和可靠性。

Method: 提出不可行性感知框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)针对minor-embedding问题提出新的数学规划公式及可证明的零相位不可行性筛选，实现可扩展的标注训练实例构建；(2)使用精确优化管线生成训练数据，对8B参数的LLM进行监督微调，使其联合执行解生成和不可行性检测；(3)利用LLM输出作为下游局部搜索的热启动，即使输出不完美也能加速优化过程。

Result: 微调后的模型在整体准确率上相比GPT-5.2提升高达30%；LLM引导的热启动相比从零开始的下游局部搜索，实现了高达2倍的加速效果。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了LLM在组合优化问题中不可行性检测的空白，证明了通过精确优化管线构建可认证数据集并微调LLM的可行性。所提出的框架不仅提升了模型的准确性，还通过热启动机制有效加速了下游优化过程，为LLM在实际组合优化问题中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored for NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems, but most existing methods emphasize feasible-instance solution generation and do not explicitly address infeasibility detection. We propose an infeasibility-aware framework that combines certifiable dataset construction, supervised fine-tuning, and LLM-assisted downstream search. For the minor-embedding problem, we introduce a new mathematical programming formulation together with provable zero-phase infeasibility screening, which enables scalable construction of training instances labeled either as feasible with structured certificates or as certifiably infeasible. Using training data generated through this exact optimization pipeline, we show that an 8B-parameter LLM can be fine-tuned to jointly perform solution generation and infeasibility detection. We further utilize LLM outputs as warm starts for downstream local search, providing a practical way to accelerate optimization even when the LLM outputs are imperfect. Experiments show that our fine-tuned model improves overall accuracy by up to 30\% over GPT-5.2; meanwhile LLM-guided warm starts provide up to $2\times$ speedup compared with starting from scratch in downstream local search.

</details>

*Daniel Xie,Maxwell J. Jacobson,Adil Wazeer,Haiyan Wang,Xinghang Zhang,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了P-COD方法，利用文档间关系来减少大语言模型的数据提取幻觉，在科学文献摘要领域实现了高达98%的异常检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的减少LLM幻觉的方法（如提示工程和思维链）主要关注单个文档，忽略了文档语料库之间的关系。为了提高大规模文本语料库数据提取的准确性，需要一种能够利用文档间关系的方法。

Method: 提出Peer Context Outlier Detection (P-COD)方法，利用文档间关系提高提取准确性。在科学文献摘要领域，假设具有相似实验设置的论文应得出相似结论。通过将提取的数据与语料库中经过验证的对等信息进行比较，调整置信度分数并标记低置信度结果供专家审查。

Result: 在6个科学领域的实验中，异常检测的精度高达98%。该方法有效减少了幻觉，增强了对自动化系统的信任，并使研究人员能够专注于模糊案例。

Conclusion: P-COD通过利用语料库中的对等验证信息，成功降低了LLM幻觉问题，提高了数据提取的可靠性和效率，为科学文献摘要等领域的自动化数据提取提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reducing hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) is essential for improving the accuracy of data extraction from large text corpora. Current methods, like prompt engineering and chain-of-thought prompting, focus on individual documents but fail to consider relationships across a corpus. This paper introduces Peer Context Outlier Detection (P-COD), a novel approach that uses the relationships between documents to improve extraction accuracy. Our application domain is in scientific literature summarization, where papers with similar experiment settings should draw similar conclusions. By comparing extracted data to validated peer information within the corpus, we adjust confidence scores and flag low-confidence results for expert review. High-confidence results, supported by peer validation, are considered reliable. Our experiments demonstrate up to 98% precision in outlier detection across 6 domains of science, demonstrating that our design reduces hallucinations, enhances trust in automated systems, and allows researchers to focus on ambiguous cases, streamlining the data extraction workflows.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个具备上下文级技能进化能力的智能体框架，用于解决超表面逆向设计中工作流构建的难题，在训练任务集上将成功率从38%提升至74%，证明了在不修改模型权重的情况下积累可复用的求解器专业知识的可行性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出了一个集成了编码智能体、进化技能工件和基于物理仿真的确定性评估器的智能体框架。该框架通过上下文级技能进化，在不修改模型权重或底层物理求解器的前提下，使特定求解器的策略能够在任务间迭代优化和复用。

Result: 在包含多类超表面逆向设计任务的基准测试上，进化的技能将分布内任务成功率从38%提升至74%，标准通过分数从0.510提升至0.870，平均尝试次数从4.10降至2.30。在未见过的任务族上，二元成功率变化不大，但最佳边距提升、错误组成变化及智能体行为转变表明工作流知识实现了部分迁移。

Conclusion: 技能进化的主要价值在于围绕可靠计算引擎积累可复用的特定求解器专业知识，这为构建更自主、更易访问的超表面逆向设计工作流提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Metasurface inverse design has become central to realizing complex optical functionality, yet translating target responses into executable, solver-compatible workflows still demands specialized expertise in computational electromagnetics and solver-specific software engineering. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer a complementary route to reducing this workflow-construction burden, but existing language-driven systems remain largely session-bounded and do not preserve reusable workflow knowledge across inverse-design tasks. We present an agentic framework for metasurface inverse design that addresses this limitation through context-level skill evolution. The framework couples a coding agent, evolving skill artifacts, and a deterministic evaluator grounded in physical simulation so that solver-specific strategies can be iteratively refined across tasks without modifying model weights or the underlying physics solver. We evaluate the framework on a benchmark spanning multiple metasurface inverse-design task types, with separate training-aligned and held-out task families. Evolved skills raise in-distribution task success from 38% to 74%, increase criteria pass fraction from 0.510 to 0.870, and reduce average attempts from 4.10 to 2.30. On held-out task families, binary success changes only marginally, but improvements in best margin together with shifts in error composition and agent behavior indicate partial transfer of workflow knowledge. These results suggest that the main value of skill evolution lies in accumulating reusable solver-specific expertise around reliable computational engines, thereby offering a practical path toward more autonomous and accessible metasurface inverse-design workflows.

</details>


### [38] [AgentSocialBench: Evaluating Privacy Risks in Human-Centered Agentic Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01487)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个以人为中心的智能体社交网络隐私风险评估基准AgentSocialBench，揭示了跨域跨用户协调导致的持续隐私泄露压力以及“抽象悖论”现象，表明现有LLM智能体缺乏稳健的隐私保护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着个性化持久LLM智能体框架（如OpenClaw）的兴起，以人为中心的智能体社交网络正在成为现实，其中协作AI智能体团队在社交网络中跨多个领域为个人用户服务。这种环境创造了新的隐私挑战：智能体必须跨领域边界协调、在人类之间进行调解、与其他用户的智能体交互，同时保护敏感个人信息。虽然先前工作已评估过多智能体协调和隐私保护，但以人为中心的智能体社交网络的动态和隐私风险仍未被探索。

Method: 引入AgentSocialBench基准，系统性地评估此环境下的隐私风险。该基准包含跨越七个类别的场景，涵盖双边和多方交互，基于具有层次敏感性标签和有向社会图的现实用户画像。通过实验评估智能体在跨域和跨用户协调场景下的隐私保护表现。

Result: 实验揭示了智能体社交网络中的隐私保护比单智能体环境本质上更困难：（1）跨域和跨用户协调产生持续的泄露压力，即使智能体被明确指示保护信息；（2）教导智能体如何抽象敏感信息的隐私指令反而导致它们更多地讨论敏感信息（作者称之为“抽象悖论”）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体缺乏在以人为中心的智能体社交网络中进行隐私保护的稳健机制，仅靠提示工程不足以解决问题，需要新的方法来确保智能体介导的社会协调在现实部署中的安全性。

Abstract: With the rise of personalized, persistent LLM agent frameworks such as OpenClaw, human-centered agentic social networks in which teams of collaborative AI agents serve individual users in a social network across multiple domains are becoming a reality. This setting creates novel privacy challenges: agents must coordinate across domain boundaries, mediate between humans, and interact with other users' agents, all while protecting sensitive personal information.
